│ <exit-code> 0 </exit-code>                                                   │
│ <output> # Formal testing function test <- function() { cat("Starting ARS    │
│ tests...\n")                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                      Test 1: Normal(0,1) on [-8,8]                       ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ dens_norm <- function(x) exp(-0.5 * x^2) a1 <- -8; b1 <- 8; n1 <- 2000       │
│ cat("TEST_NORM...\n") set.seed(123) # for reproducibility samp_norm <-       │
│ tryCatch({ ars(dens_norm, a1, b1, n1) }, error = function(e) e) if           │
│ (inherits(samp_norm, "error")) { cat("TEST_NORM: FAIL (error)\n") } else {   │
│ write.table(samp_norm, file = "/app/normal_samples.txt", row.names = FALSE,  │
│ col.names = FALSE) m1 <- mean(samp_norm); s1 <- sd(samp_norm)                │
│ cat(sprintf("TEST_NORM: mean=%.4f sd=%.4f\n", m1, s1)) ok1 <- (abs(m1) <     │
│ 0.15) & (abs(s1 - 1) < 0.25) if (ok1) cat("TEST_NORM: PASS\n") else          │
│ cat("TEST_NORM: FAIL\n") }                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                     Test 2: Exponential(1) on [0,20]                     ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ dens_exp <- function(x) exp(-x) a2 <- 0; b2 <- 20; n2 <- 2000                │

│ finite\n    - n is a positive integer\n    - init_pts is >= 2\n  - Builds an │
│ initial hull with init_pts points evenly spaced in [a, b]\n  - Evaluates     │

│ tangent lines and their intersections z\n  - Pre-computes weights for        │
│ sampling from the envelope\n  - Repeats sampling from the envelope and       │
│ accepts/rejects based on dens\n  - Returns a vector of length n with         │
│ samples\n  - Notes:\n    - The current version does not aggressively rebuild │
│ the hull after each accepted sample (comment indicates where you could       │
│ extend with hull updates to improve efficiency)\n- test()\n  - Runs three    │

│ expected to raise an error\n  - Seeds RNG to improve reproducibility\n  -    │

│ /app/ars.R\n# Robust, modular ARS with input validation, log-concavity       │

│ init_pts = 5,\n                max_iter = 20000,\n                           │
│ max_sup_pts = 60,\n                update_every = 5) {\n  # Basic input      │
│ validation\n  if (!is.function(dens)) stop(&quot;dens must be a function of  │
│ x.&quot;)\n  if (!is.finite(a) || !is.finite(b) || a &gt;= b)                │
│ stop(&quot;Domain [a,b] must be finite with a &lt; b.&quot;)\n  if           │
│ (!is.numeric(n) || length(n) != 1 || n &lt;= 0) stop(&quot;n must be a       │
│ positive integer.&quot;)\n  if (!is.numeric(init_pts) || length(init_pts) != │
│ 1 || init_pts &lt; 2) stop(&quot;init_pts must be &gt;= 2.&quot;)\n  if      │
│ (!is.numeric(max_iter) || length(max_iter) != 1 || max_iter &lt;= 0)         │
│ stop(&quot;max_iter must be positive.&quot;)\n  if (!is.numeric(max_sup_pts) │
│ || length(max_sup_pts) != 1 || max_sup_pts &lt; 2) stop(&quot;max_sup_pts    │
│ must be &gt;= 2.&quot;)\n  if (!is.numeric(update_every) ||                  │
│ length(update_every) != 1 || update_every &lt; 1) stop(&quot;update_every    │
│ must be &gt;= 1.&quot;)\n  n &lt;- as.integer(n)\n  init_pts &lt;-           │
│ as.integer(init_pts)\n  update_every &lt;- as.integer(update_every)\n\n  #   │
│ Helper: rebuild hull from support points\n  rebuild_hull &lt;-               │
│ function(s_pts) {\n    # Evaluate log-density on support points\n    fvals   │
│ &lt;- dens(s_pts)\n    if (any(!is.finite(fvals)) || any(is.na(fvals)) ||    │
│ any(fvals &lt;= 0)) {\n      stop(&quot;dens must be positive and finite on  │
│ all hull points.&quot;)\n    }\n    h &lt;- log(fvals)\n\n    m &lt;-        │
│ length(s_pts)\n    if (m &lt; 2) stop(&quot;Need at least two support        │
│ points.&quot;)\n\n    # Slopes h\&#x27; (finite differences)\n    hp &lt;-   │
│ numeric(m)\n    if (m &gt;= 3) {\n      for (i in 2:(m-1)) hp[i] &lt;-       │
│ (h[i+1] - h[i-1]) / (s_pts[i+1] - s_pts[i-1])\n    }\n    hp[1] &lt;- (h[2]  │
│ - h[1]) / (s_pts[2] - s_pts[1])\n    hp[m] &lt;- (h[m] - h[m-1]) / (s_pts[m] │
│ - s_pts[m-1])\n\n    # Check log-concavity: h\&#x27;\&#x27; &lt;= 0 on       │
│ interior\n    hpp &lt;- numeric(m)\n    if (m &gt;= 3) {\n      for (i in    │
│ 2:(m-1)) hpp[i] &lt;- (hp[i+1] - hp[i-1]) / (s_pts[i+1] - s_pts[i-1])\n      │
│ }\n    tol &lt;- 1e-12\n    if (any(hpp[2:(m-1)] &gt; tol, na.rm = TRUE))    │
│ stop(&quot;Density is not log-concave on the current hull.&quot;)\n    \n    │
│ B &lt;- h - hp * s_pts\n    z &lt;- numeric(m + 1)\n    z[1] &lt;- a\n       │
│ z[m+1] &lt;- b\n    if (m &gt;= 2) {\n      for (i in 1:(m-1)) {\n        Ai │
│ &lt;- hp[i]; Bi &lt;- B[i]\n        Aj &lt;- hp[i+1]; Bj &lt;- B[i+1]\n      │
│ if (abs(Ai - Aj) &lt; 1e-14) {\n          z[i+1] &lt;- (s_pts[i] +           │
│ s_pts[i+1]) / 2\n        } else {\n          z[i+1] &lt;- (Bj - Bi) / (Ai -  │

│ stop(&quot;Non-monotone hull z; density may not be log-concave on            │
│ domain.&quot;)\n    \n    # Weights for sampling from envelope\n    w &lt;-  │
│ numeric(m)\n    for (i in 1:m) {\n      xl &lt;- z[i]; xr &lt;- z[i+1]\n     │
│ Ai &lt;- hp[i]; Di &lt;- B[i]\n      if (abs(Ai) &lt; 1e-12) {\n        w[i] │

│ e)\n  if (inherits(hull, &quot;error&quot;)) stop(paste(&quot;Initial hull   │
│ construction failed:&quot;, conditionMessage(hull)))\n  samples &lt;-        │
│ numeric(n)\n  k &lt;- 0L\n  iter &lt;- 0L\n  tolA &lt;- 1e-12\n\n  repeat    │
│ {\n    if (k &gt;= n) break\n    iter &lt;- iter + 1L\n    if (iter &gt;     │
│ max_iter) stop(&quot;ARS failed to generate enough samples within            │
│ max_iter.&quot;)\n\n    # Sample a segment according to weights\n    u &lt;- │
│ runif(1, min = 0, max = hull$total_w)\n    cum &lt;- 0\n    seg &lt;-        │
│ length(hull$s) - 1\n    for (i in 1:length(hull$s)) {\n      cum &lt;- cum + │
│ hull$w[i]\n      if (u &lt;= cum) {\n        seg &lt;- i\n        break\n    │
│ }\n    }\n    xl &lt;- hull$z[seg]; xr &lt;- hull$z[seg+1]\n    Ai &lt;-     │
│ hull$hp[seg]; Di &lt;- hull$B[seg]\n\n    # Sample from exp(l_i) on [xl,     │
│ xr]\n    if (abs(Ai) &gt; tolA) {\n      u2 &lt;- runif(1)\n      x &lt;-    │
│ (log(u2 * (exp(Ai * xr + Di) - exp(Ai * xl + Di)) + exp(Ai * xl + Di))) /    │
│ Ai\n    } else {\n      x &lt;- runif(1, xl, xr)\n    }\n\n    if            │
│ (!is.finite(x) || x &lt; a || x &gt; b) next\n    fx &lt;- dens(x)\n    if   │
│ (!is.finite(fx) || fx &lt;= 0) next\n    hx &lt;- log(fx)\n    if            │
│ (log(runif(1)) &lt;= hx - (Ai * x + Di)) {\n      k &lt;- k + 1L\n           │
│ samples[k] &lt;- x\n\n      # Optional: dynamic hull update\n      if        │
│ (length(hull$s) &lt; max_sup_pts &amp;&amp; (k %% update_every) == 0) {\n    │
│ new_s &lt;- sort(unique(c(hull$s, x)))\n        hull &lt;- tryCatch({        │
│ rebuild_hull(new_s) }, error = function(e) hull)\n        # If rebuild fails │
│ due to non-log-concavity (rare), keep old hull\n        if (inherits(hull,   │
│ &quot;error&quot;)) hull &lt;- hull  # keep old hull\n      }\n    }\n       │
│ }\n\n  if (k &lt; n) stop(&quot;ARS failed to generate enough samples within │
│ max_iter.&quot;)\n  samples[1:n]\n}\n\n# Formal testing function\ntest &lt;- │
│ function() {\n  cat(&quot;Starting ARS tests...\\n&quot;)\n\n  # Test 1:     │
│ Normal(0,1) on [-8,8]\n  dens_norm &lt;- function(x) exp(-0.5 * x^2)\n  a1   │
│ &lt;- -8; b1 &lt;- 8; n1 &lt;- 2000\n  cat(&quot;TEST_NORM...\\n&quot;)\n    │
│ set.seed(123)\n  res1 &lt;- tryCatch({ ars(dens_norm, a1, b1, n1) }, error = │
│ function(e) e)\n  if (inherits(res1, &quot;error&quot;)) {\n                 │
│ cat(&quot;TEST_NORM: FAIL (error)\\n&quot;)\n  } else {\n                    │
│ write.table(res1, file = &quot;/app/normal_samples.txt&quot;, row.names =    │
│ FALSE, col.names = FALSE)\n    m1 &lt;- mean(res1); s1 &lt;- sd(res1)\n      │
│ cat(sprintf(&quot;TEST_NORM: mean=%.4f sd=%.4f\\n&quot;, m1, s1))\n    ok1   │
│ &lt;- (abs(m1) &lt; 0.15) &amp; (abs(s1 - 1) &lt; 0.25)\n    if (ok1)        │
│ cat(&quot;TEST_NORM: PASS\\n&quot;) else cat(&quot;TEST_NORM:                │
│ FAIL\\n&quot;)\n  }\n\n  # Test 2: Exponential(1) on [0,20]\n  dens_exp      │
│ &lt;- function(x) exp(-x)\n  a2 &lt;- 0; b2 &lt;- 20; n2 &lt;- 2000\n        │
│ cat(&quot;TEST_EXP...\\n&quot;)\n  set.seed(456)\n  res2 &lt;- tryCatch({    │
│ ars(dens_exp, a2, b2, n2) }, error = function(e) e)\n  if (inherits(res2,    │
│ &quot;error&quot;)) {\n    cat(&quot;TEST_EXP: FAIL (error)\\n&quot;)\n  }   │

│ &quot;/app/exponential_samples.txt&quot;, row.names = FALSE, col.names =     │
│ FALSE)\n    m2 &lt;- mean(res2); s2 &lt;- sd(res2)\n                         │
│ cat(sprintf(&quot;TEST_EXP: mean=%.4f sd=%.4f\\n&quot;, m2, s2))\n    ok2    │
│ &lt;- (abs(m2 - 1) &lt; 0.15) &amp; (abs(s2 - 1) &lt; 0.25)\n    if (ok2)    │
│ cat(&quot;TEST_EXP: PASS\\n&quot;) else cat(&quot;TEST_EXP: FAIL\\n&quot;)\n │
│ }\n\n  # Test 3: Non-log-concave detector (should error)\n  dens_nlc &lt;-   │

│                                                                              │
│ Code block (complete, self-contained):                                       │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  # Adaptive Rejection Sampling (ARS) - self-contained /app/ars.R             │
│  # Robust, modular ARS with input validation, log-concavity checks, and      │
│  optional hull updates                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  ars &lt;- function(dens, a, b, n,                                           │
│                  init_pts = 5,                                               │
│                  max_iter = 20000,                                           │
│                  max_sup_pts = 60,                                           │
│                  update_every = 5) {                                         │
│    # Basic input validation                                                  │
│    if (!is.function(dens)) stop(&quot;dens must be a function of x.&quot;)   │
│    if (!is.finite(a) || !is.finite(b) || a &gt;= b) stop(&quot;Domain [a,b]  │
│  must be finite with a &lt; b.&quot;)                                        │
│    if (!is.numeric(n) || length(n) != 1 || n &lt;= 0) stop(&quot;n must be   │
│  a positive integer.&quot;)                                                  │
│    if (!is.numeric(init_pts) || length(init_pts) != 1 || init_pts &lt; 2)    │
│  stop(&quot;init_pts must be &gt;= 2.&quot;)                                 │
│    if (!is.numeric(max_iter) || length(max_iter) != 1 || max_iter &lt;= 0)   │
│  stop(&quot;max_iter must be positive.&quot;)                                │
│    if (!is.numeric(max_sup_pts) || length(max_sup_pts) != 1 || max_sup_pts   │
│  &lt; 2) stop(&quot;max_sup_pts must be &gt;= 2.&quot;)                      │
│    if (!is.numeric(update_every) || length(update_every) != 1 ||             │
│  update_every &lt; 1) stop(&quot;update_every must be &gt;= 1.&quot;)        │
│    n &lt;- as.integer(n)                                                     │
│    init_pts &lt;- as.integer(init_pts)                                       │
│    update_every &lt;- as.integer(update_every)                               │
│                                                                              │
│    # Helper: rebuild hull from support points                                │
│    rebuild_hull &lt;- function(s_pts) {                                      │
│      # Evaluate log-density on support points                                │
│      fvals &lt;- dens(s_pts)                                                 │
│      if (any(!is.finite(fvals)) || any(is.na(fvals)) || any(fvals &lt;= 0))  │
│  {                                                                           │
│        stop(&quot;dens must be positive and finite on all hull               │
│  points.&quot;)                                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│      h &lt;- log(fvals)                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│      m &lt;- length(s_pts)                                                   │
│      if (m &lt; 2) stop(&quot;Need at least two support points.&quot;)       │
│                                                                              │
│      # Slopes h&#x27; (finite differences)                                   │
│      hp &lt;- numeric(m)                                                     │
│      if (m &gt;= 3) {                                                        │
│        for (i in 2:(m-1)) hp[i] &lt;- (h[i+1] - h[i-1]) / (s_pts[i+1] -      │
│  s_pts[i-1])                                                                 │
│      }                                                                       │
│      hp[1] &lt;- (h[2] - h[1]) / (s_pts[2] - s_pts[1])                       │
│      hp[m] &lt;- (h[m] - h[m-1]) / (s_pts[m] - s_pts[m-1])                   │
│                                                                              │
│      # Check log-concavity: h&#x27;&#x27; &lt;= 0 on interior                │
│      hpp &lt;- numeric(m)                                                    │
│      if (m &gt;= 3) {                                                        │
│        for (i in 2:(m-1)) hpp[i] &lt;- (hp[i+1] - hp[i-1]) / (s_pts[i+1] -   │
│  s_pts[i-1])                                                                 │
│      }                                                                       │
│      tol &lt;- 1e-12                                                         │
│      if (any(hpp[2:(m-1)] &gt; tol, na.rm = TRUE)) stop(&quot;Density is     │
│  not log-concave on the current hull.&quot;)                                 │
│                                                                              │
│      B &lt;- h - hp * s_pts                                                  │
│      z &lt;- numeric(m + 1)                                                  │
│      z[1] &lt;- a                                                            │
│      z[m+1] &lt;- b                                                          │
│      if (m &gt;= 2) {                                                        │
│        for (i in 1:(m-1)) {                                                  │
│          Ai &lt;- hp[i]; Bi &lt;- B[i]                                       │
│          Aj &lt;- hp[i+1]; Bj &lt;- B[i+1]                                   │
│          if (abs(Ai - Aj) &lt; 1e-14) {                                      │
│            z[i+1] &lt;- (s_pts[i] + s_pts[i+1]) / 2                          │
│          } else {                                                            │
│            z[i+1] &lt;- (Bj - Bi) / (Ai - Aj)                                │
│          }                                                                   │
│        }                                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      if (any(diff(z) &lt;= 0)) stop(&quot;Non-monotone hull z; density may   │
│  not be log-concave on domain.&quot;)                                        │
│                                                                              │
│      # Weights for sampling from envelope                                    │
│      w &lt;- numeric(m)                                                      │
│      for (i in 1:m) {                                                        │
│        xl &lt;- z[i]; xr &lt;- z[i+1]                                        │
│        Ai &lt;- hp[i]; Di &lt;- B[i]                                         │
│        if (abs(Ai) &lt; 1e-12) {                                             │
│          w[i] &lt;- exp(Di) * (xr - xl)                                      │
│        } else {                                                              │
│          w[i] &lt;- (exp(Ai * xr + Di) - exp(Ai * xl + Di)) / Ai             │
│        }                                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      total_w &lt;- sum(w)                                                    │
│      if (total_w &lt;= 0) stop(&quot;Envelope has non-positive total         │
│  mass.&quot;)                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│      list(s = s_pts, w = w, total_w = total_w, z = z, hp = hp, B = B, h =    │
│  h,                                                                          │
│           fvals = fvals, hvals = h, a = a, b = b)                            │
│    }                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│    # Initialize hull with initial points                                     │
│    s &lt;- seq(a, b, length.out = init_pts)                                  │
│    hull &lt;- tryCatch({ rebuild_hull(s) }, error = function(e) e)           │
│    if (inherits(hull, &quot;error&quot;)) stop(paste(&quot;Initial hull      │
│  construction failed:&quot;, conditionMessage(hull)))                        │
│    samples &lt;- numeric(n)                                                  │
│    k &lt;- 0L                                                                │
│    iter &lt;- 0L                                                             │
│    tolA &lt;- 1e-12                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│    repeat {                                                                  │
│      if (k &gt;= n) break                                                    │
│      iter &lt;- iter + 1L                                                    │
│      if (iter &gt; max_iter) stop(&quot;ARS failed to generate enough        │
│  samples within max_iter.&quot;)                                             │
│                                                                              │
│      # Sample a segment according to weights                                 │
│      u &lt;- runif(1, min = 0, max = hull$total_w)                           │
│      cum &lt;- 0                                                             │
│      seg &lt;- length(hull$s) - 1                                            │
│      for (i in 1:length(hull$s)) {                                           │
│        cum &lt;- cum + hull$w[i]                                             │
│        if (u &lt;= cum) {                                                    │
│          seg &lt;- i                                                         │
│          break                                                               │
│        }                                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      xl &lt;- hull$z[seg]; xr &lt;- hull$z[seg+1]                            │
│      Ai &lt;- hull$hp[seg]; Di &lt;- hull$B[seg]                             │
│                                                                              │
│      # Sample from exp(l_i) on [xl, xr]                                      │
│      if (abs(Ai) &gt; tolA) {                                                │
│        u2 &lt;- runif(1)                                                     │
│        x &lt;- (log(u2 * (exp(Ai * xr + Di) - exp(Ai * xl + Di)) + exp(Ai *  │
│  xl + Di))) / Ai                                                             │
│      } else {                                                                │
│        x &lt;- runif(1, xl, xr)                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      if (!is.finite(x) || x &lt; a || x &gt; b) next                         │
│      fx &lt;- dens(x)                                                        │
│      if (!is.finite(fx) || fx &lt;= 0) next                                  │
│      hx &lt;- log(fx)                                                        │
│      if (log(runif(1)) &lt;= hx - (Ai * x + Di)) {                           │
│        k &lt;- k + 1L                                                        │
│        samples[k] &lt;- x                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│        # Optional: dynamic hull update                                       │
│        if (length(hull$s) &lt; max_sup_pts &amp;&amp; (k %% update_every)    │
│  == 0) {                                                                     │
│          new_s &lt;- sort(unique(c(hull$s, x)))                              │
│          hull &lt;- tryCatch({ rebuild_hull(new_s) }, error = function(e)    │
│  hull)                                                                       │
│          # If rebuild fails due to non-log-concavity (rare), keep old hull   │
│          if (inherits(hull, &quot;error&quot;)) hull &lt;- hull  # keep old  │
│  hull                                                                        │
│        }                                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│    }                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│    if (k &lt; n) stop(&quot;ARS failed to generate enough samples within     │
│  max_iter.&quot;)                                                            │
│    samples[1:n]                                                              │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  # Formal testing function                                                   │
│  test &lt;- function() {                                                     │
│    cat(&quot;Starting ARS tests...\n&quot;)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    # Test 1: Normal(0,1) on [-8,8]                                           │
│    dens_norm &lt;- function(x) exp(-0.5 * x^2)                               │
│    a1 &lt;- -8; b1 &lt;- 8; n1 &lt;- 2000                                    │
│    cat(&quot;TEST_NORM...\n&quot;)                                           │
│    set.seed(123)                                                             │
│    res1 &lt;- tryCatch({ ars(dens_norm, a1, b1, n1) }, error = function(e)   │
│  e)                                                                          │
│    if (inherits(res1, &quot;error&quot;)) {                                  │
│      cat(&quot;TEST_NORM: FAIL (error)\n&quot;)                              │
│    } else {                                                                  │
│      write.table(res1, file = &quot;/app/normal_samples.txt&quot;,           │
│  row.names = FALSE, col.names = FALSE)                                       │
│      m1 &lt;- mean(res1); s1 &lt;- sd(res1)                                  │
│      cat(sprintf(&quot;TEST_NORM: mean=%.4f sd=%.4f\n&quot;, m1, s1))        │
│      ok1 &lt;- (abs(m1) &lt; 0.15) &amp; (abs(s1 - 1) &lt; 0.25)             │
│      if (ok1) cat(&quot;TEST_NORM: PASS\n&quot;) else cat(&quot;TEST_NORM:   │
│  FAIL\n&quot;)                                                               │
│    }                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│    # Test 2: Exponential(1) on [0,20]                                        │
│    dens_exp &lt;- function(x) exp(-x)                                        │
│    a2 &lt;- 0; b2 &lt;- 20; n2 &lt;- 2000                                    │
│    cat(&quot;TEST_EXP...\n&quot;)                                            │
│    set.seed(456)                                                             │
│    res2 &lt;- tryCatch({ ars(dens_exp, a2, b2, n2) }, error = function(e)    │
│  e)                                                                          │
│    if (inherits(res2, &quot;error&quot;)) {                                  │
│      cat(&quot;TEST_EXP: FAIL (error)\n&quot;)                               │
│    } else {                                                                  │
│      write.table(res2, file = &quot;/app/exponential_samples.txt&quot;,      │

│      cat(sprintf(&quot;TEST_EXP: mean=%.4f sd=%.4f\n&quot;, m2, s2))         │
│      ok2 &lt;- (abs(m2 - 1) &lt; 0.15) &amp; (abs(s2 - 1) &lt; 0.25)         │
│      if (ok2) cat(&quot;TEST_EXP: PASS\n&quot;) else cat(&quot;TEST_EXP:     │
│  FAIL\n&quot;)                                                               │
│    }                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│    # Test 3: Non-log-concave detector (should error)                         │
│    dens_nlc &lt;- function(x) 0.5 * dnorm(x, mean = 2, sd = 1) + 0.5 *       │
│  dnorm(x, mean = -2, sd = 1)                                                 │

│    cat(&quot;TEST_NLC...\n&quot;)                                            │

│    res3 &lt;- tryCatch({ ars(dens_nlc, a3, b3, n3) }, error = function(e)    │

│    if (inherits(res3, &quot;error&quot;)) {                                  │
│      cat(&quot;TEST_NLC: PASS\n&quot;)                                       │
│    } else {                                                                  │
│      cat(&quot;TEST_NLC: FAIL (expected error)\n&quot;)                      │

│  # End of file                                                               │

│ Notes                                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│  • This ARS operates on a finite domain [a, b] and includes optional hull    │
│    updates after accepted samples. It checks log-concavity on the active     │
│    hull and will raise errors if non-log-concavity is observed.              │
│  • The test() routine seeds RNG for reproducibility and writes:              │
│     • /app/normal_samples.txt                                                │
│     • /app/exponential_samples.txt                                           │
│  • If you prefer tighter or looser tolerances for the tests, you can adjust  │
│    the threshold values in test() for mean/sd checks (currently 0.15 for     │
│    |mean| and 0.25 for |sd-1| for the normal; 0.15 for mean and 0.25 for sd  │
│    for the exponential).                                                     │

│ <- numeric(m)\n    if (m >= 3) {\n      for (i in 2:(m-1)) hp[i] <- (h[i+1]  │

│ hpp <- numeric(m)\n    if (m >= 3) {\n      for (i in 2:(m-1)) hpp[i] <-     │
│ (hp[i+1] - hp[i-1]) / (s_pts[i+1] - s_pts[i-1])\n    }\n    tol <- 1e-12\n   │
│ if (any(hpp[2:(m-1)] > tol, na.rm = TRUE)) stop(\"Density is not log-concave │
│ on the current hull.\")\n    \n    B <- h - hp * s_pts\n    z <- numeric(m + │
│ 1)\n    z[1] <- a\n    z[m+1] <- b\n    if (m >= 2) {\n      for (i in       │
│ 1:(m-1)) {\n        Ai <- hp[i]; Bi <- B[i]\n        Aj <- hp[i+1]; Bj <-    │
│ B[i+1]\n        if (abs(Ai - Aj) < 1e-14) {\n          z[i+1] <- (s_pts[i] + │

│ # Initialize hull with initial points\n  s <- seq(a, b, length.out =         │

│ if (iter > max_iter) stop(\"ARS failed to generate enough samples within     │
│ max_iter.\")\n    # Sample a segment according to weights\n    u <- runif(1) │
│ * hull$total_w\n    cum <- 0\n    seg <- 1\n    for (i in 1:length(hull$s))  │
│ {\n      cum <- cum + hull$w[i]\n      if (u <= cum) { seg <- i; break }\n   │

│ tolA) {\n      u2 <- runif(1)\n      x <- (log(u2 * (exp(Ai * xr + Di) -     │
│ exp(Ai * xl + Di)) + exp(Ai * xl + Di))) / Ai\n    } else {\n      x <-      │
│ runif(1, xl, xr)\n    }\n    if (!is.finite(x) || x < a || x > b) next\n     │
│ fx <- dens(x)\n    if (!is.finite(fx) || fx <= 0) next\n    hx <- log(fx)\n  │
│ if (log(runif(1)) <= hx - (Ai * x + Di)) {\n      k <- k + 1L\n              │
│ samples[k] <- x\n      # Optional hull update\n      if (length(hull$s) <    │
│ max_sup_pts && (k %% update_every) == 0) {\n        new_s <-                 │
│ sort(unique(c(hull$s, x)))\n        hull <- tryCatch({ rebuild_hull(new_s)   │
│ }, error = function(e) hull)\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  samples[1:n]\n}\n\n#    │

│ # Helper: rebuild hull from a set of support points\n  rebuild_hull &lt;-    │
│ function(s_pts) {\n    fvals &lt;- dens(s_pts)\n    if                       │
│ (any(!is.finite(fvals)) || any(is.na(fvals)) || any(fvals &lt;= 0)) {\n      │
│ stop(&quot;dens must be positive and finite on all hull points.&quot;)\n     │
│ }\n    h &lt;- log(fvals)\n\n    m &lt;- length(s_pts)\n    if (m &lt; 2)    │
│ stop(&quot;Need at least two support points.&quot;)\n\n    # h\&#x27; (slope │
│ of log-density) via finite differences\n    hp &lt;- numeric(m)\n    if (m   │
│ &gt;= 3) {\n      for (i in 2:(m-1)) hp[i] &lt;- (h[i+1] - h[i-1]) /         │
│ (s_pts[i+1] - s_pts[i-1])\n    }\n    hp[1] &lt;- (h[2] - h[1]) / (s_pts[2]  │

│ 1e-12\n    if (any(hpp[2:(m-1)] &gt; tol, na.rm = TRUE)) stop(&quot;Density  │
│ is not log-concave on the current hull.&quot;)\n    \n    B &lt;- h - hp *   │
│ s_pts\n    z &lt;- numeric(m + 1)\n    z[1] &lt;- a\n    z[m+1] &lt;- b\n    │
│ if (m &gt;= 2) {\n      for (i in 1:(m-1)) {\n        Ai &lt;- hp[i]; Bi     │
│ &lt;- B[i]\n        Aj &lt;- hp[i+1]; Bj &lt;- B[i+1]\n        if (abs(Ai -  │
│ Aj) &lt; 1e-14) {\n          z[i+1] &lt;- (s_pts[i] + s_pts[i+1]) / 2\n      │
│ } else {\n          z[i+1] &lt;- (Bj - Bi) / (Ai - Aj)\n        }\n      }\n │
│ }\n    if (any(diff(z) &lt;= 0)) stop(&quot;Non-monotone hull z; density may │

│ envelope\n    w &lt;- numeric(m)\n    for (i in 1:m) {\n      xl &lt;- z[i]; │

│ w[i] &lt;- (exp(Ai * xr + Di) - exp(Ai * xl + Di)) / Ai\n      }\n    }\n    │
│ total_w &lt;- sum(w)\n    if (total_w &lt;= 0) stop(&quot;Envelope has       │

│ Initialize hull with initial points\n  s &lt;- seq(a, b, length.out =        │
│ init_pts)\n  hull &lt;- tryCatch({ rebuild_hull(s) }, error = function(e)    │
│ e)\n  if (inherits(hull, &quot;error&quot;)) stop(paste(&quot;Initial hull   │
│ construction failed:&quot;, conditionMessage(hull)))\n\n  samples &lt;-      │
│ numeric(n)\n  k &lt;- 0L\n  iter &lt;- 0L\n  tolA &lt;- 1e-12\n\n  while (k  │
│ &lt; n) {\n    iter &lt;- iter + 1L\n    if (iter &gt; max_iter)             │
│ stop(&quot;ARS failed to generate enough samples within max_iter.&quot;)\n\n │
│ # Sample a segment according to weights\n    u &lt;- runif(1, min = 0, max = │
│ hull$total_w)\n    cum &lt;- 0\n    seg &lt;- length(hull$s) - 1\n    for (i │
│ in 1:(length(hull$s))) {\n      cum &lt;- cum + hull$w[i]\n      if (u &lt;= │
│ cum) { seg &lt;- i; break }\n    }\n    xl &lt;- hull$z[seg]; xr &lt;-       │
│ hull$z[seg+1]\n    Ai &lt;- hull$hp[seg]; Di &lt;- hull$B[seg]\n\n    #      │
│ Sample from exp(l_i) on [xl, xr]\n    if (abs(Ai) &gt; tolA) {\n      u2     │
│ &lt;- runif(1)\n      x &lt;- (log(u2 * (exp(Ai * xr + Di) - exp(Ai * xl +   │
│ Di)) + exp(Ai * xl + Di))) / Ai\n    } else {\n      x &lt;- runif(1, xl,    │
│ xr)\n    }\n\n    if (!is.finite(x) || x &lt; a || x &gt; b) next\n    fx    │
│ &lt;- dens(x)\n    if (!is.finite(fx) || fx &lt;= 0) next\n    hx &lt;-      │
│ log(fx)\n    if (log(runif(1)) &lt;= hx - (Ai * x + Di)) {\n      k &lt;- k  │
│ + 1L\n      samples[k] &lt;- x\n\n      # Optional: update hull with this    │
│ accepted sample\n      if (length(hull$s) &lt; max_sup_pts &amp;&amp; (k %%  │
│ update_every) == 0) {\n        new_s &lt;- sort(unique(c(hull$s, x)))\n      │
│ hull &lt;- tryCatch({ rebuild_hull(new_s) }, error = function(e) hull)\n     │
│ # If rebuild fails due to non-log-concavity (rare), keep old hull\n          │
│ if (inherits(hull, &quot;error&quot;)) hull &lt;- hull\n      }\n    }\n     │
│ }\n\n  if (k &lt; n) stop(&quot;ARS failed to generate enough samples within │
│ max_iter.&quot;)\n  samples[1:n]\n}\n\n# Formal testing function\ntest &lt;- │
│ function() {\n  cat(&quot;Starting ARS tests...\\n&quot;)\n\n  # Test 1:     │

│ cat(sprintf(&quot;TEST_NORM: mean=%.4f sd=%.4f\\n&quot;, m1, s1))\n    ok1   │

│ &lt;- (abs(m2 - 1) &lt; 0.15) &amp; (abs(s2 - 1) &lt; 0.25)\n    if (ok2)    │
│ cat(&quot;TEST_EXP: PASS\\n&quot;) else cat(&quot;TEST_EXP: FAIL\\n&quot;)\n │
│ }\n\n  # Test 3: Non-log-concave detector (expected error)\n  dens_nlc &lt;- │
│ function(x) 0.5 * dnorm(x, mean = 2, sd = 1) + 0.5 * dnorm(x, mean = -2, sd  │
│ = 1)\n  a3 &lt;- -6; b3 &lt;- 6; n3 &lt;- 1000\n                             │
│ cat(&quot;TEST_NLC...\\n&quot;)\n  set.seed(789)\n  res3 &lt;- tryCatch({    │
│ ars(dens_nlc, a3, b3, n3) }, error = function(e) e)\n  if (inherits(res3,    │
│ &quot;error&quot;)) {\n    cat(&quot;TEST_NLC: PASS\\n&quot;)\n  } else {\n  │
│ cat(&quot;TEST_NLC: FAIL (expected error)\\n&quot;)\n  }\n}\n# End of        │
│ file\n\n\nHow to use\n- Save as /app/ars.R\n- In R:\n  -                     │
│ source("/app/ars.R")\n  - test()\n\nNotes\n- This version uses a finite      │
│ domain [a, b], with hull updates after a few accepted samples to improve     │
│ efficiency.\n- If you’d like to test full real-line targets (e.g., normal on │
│ (-Inf, Inf)), we can extend with semi-infinite tails or a                    │
│ transformation-based approach.\n- If you’d prefer a simpler, non-updating    │
│ version, I can provide that as a smaller variant as well.\n\nIf you want me  │
│ to adjust any specifics (domain choices, tolerances, or number of initial    │
│ hull points), tell me and I’ll tailor it.'                                   │

│ logprobs=None thought=None                                                   │

╭────────────────────────────── solver - message ──────────────────────────────╮
│ Absolutely. Here is a single-block, copy-paste-ready ARS implementation for  │
│ /app/ars.R that you can save directly. It includes:                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • ars(dens, a, b, n, init_pts = 5, max_iter = 20000, max_sup_pts = 60,      │
│    update_every = 5)                                                         │
│     • Validates inputs                                                       │
│     • Builds and maintains an adaptive tangent-based upper envelope          │
│     • Checks log-concavity on the current hull                               │
│     • Samples from the envelope and accepts/rejects against dens             │
│     • Optional hull updates by inserting accepted samples, up to max_sup_pts │
│  • test() routine                                                            │
│     • Normal(0,1) on [-8,8], writes /app/normal_samples.txt                  │
│     • Exponential(1) on [0,20], writes /app/exponential_samples.txt          │
│     • Non-log-concave detector test (expects an error)                       │
│     • Prints results as: TEST_NAME: PASS or TEST_NAME: FAIL with mean and sd │
│       where relevant                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ Code (complete, self-contained):                                             │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  # Adaptive Rejection Sampling (ARS) - self-contained /app/ars.R             │
│  # Robust, modular ARS with input validation, log-concavity checks, and      │
│  optional hull updates                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  ars &lt;- function(dens, a, b, n,                                           │
│                  init_pts = 5,                                               │
│                  max_iter = 20000,                                           │
│                  max_sup_pts = 60,                                           │
│                  update_every = 5) {                                         │
│    # Basic input validation                                                  │
│    if (!is.function(dens)) stop(&quot;dens must be a function of x.&quot;)   │
│    if (!is.finite(a) || !is.finite(b) || a &gt;= b) stop(&quot;Domain [a,b]  │
│  must be finite with a &lt; b.&quot;)                                        │
│    if (!is.numeric(n) || length(n) != 1 || n &lt;= 0) stop(&quot;n must be   │
│  a positive integer.&quot;)                                                  │
│    if (!is.numeric(init_pts) || length(init_pts) != 1 || init_pts &lt; 2)    │
│  stop(&quot;init_pts must be &gt;= 2.&quot;)                                 │
│    if (!is.numeric(max_iter) || length(max_iter) != 1 || max_iter &lt;= 0)   │
│  stop(&quot;max_iter must be positive.&quot;)                                │
│    if (!is.numeric(max_sup_pts) || length(max_sup_pts) != 1 || max_sup_pts   │
│  &lt; 2) stop(&quot;max_sup_pts must be &gt;= 2.&quot;)                      │
│    if (!is.numeric(update_every) || length(update_every) != 1 ||             │
│  update_every &lt; 1) stop(&quot;update_every must be &gt;= 1.&quot;)        │
│    n &lt;- as.integer(n)                                                     │
│    init_pts &lt;- as.integer(init_pts)                                       │
│    max_sup_pts &lt;- as.integer(max_sup_pts)                                 │
│    update_every &lt;- as.integer(update_every)                               │
│                                                                              │
│    # Helper: rebuild hull from a set of support points                       │
│    rebuild_hull &lt;- function(s_pts) {                                      │
│      fvals &lt;- dens(s_pts)                                                 │
│      if (any(!is.finite(fvals)) || any(is.na(fvals)) || any(fvals &lt;= 0))  │
│  {                                                                           │
│        stop(&quot;dens must be positive and finite on all hull               │
│  points.&quot;)                                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│      h &lt;- log(fvals)                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│      m &lt;- length(s_pts)                                                   │
│      if (m &lt; 2) stop(&quot;Need at least two support points.&quot;)       │
│                                                                              │
│      # h&#x27; (slope of log-density) via finite differences                 │
│      hp &lt;- numeric(m)                                                     │
│      if (m &gt;= 3) {                                                        │
│        for (i in 2:(m-1)) hp[i] &lt;- (h[i+1] - h[i-1]) / (s_pts[i+1] -      │
│  s_pts[i-1])                                                                 │
│      }                                                                       │
│      hp[1] &lt;- (h[2] - h[1]) / (s_pts[2] - s_pts[1])                       │
│      hp[m] &lt;- (h[m] - h[m-1]) / (s_pts[m] - s_pts[m-1])                   │
│                                                                              │
│      # Check log-concavity: h&#x27;&#x27; &lt;= 0 on interior                │
│      hpp &lt;- numeric(m)                                                    │
│      if (m &gt;= 3) {                                                        │
│        for (i in 2:(m-1)) hpp[i] &lt;- (hp[i+1] - hp[i-1]) / (s_pts[i+1] -   │
│  s_pts[i-1])                                                                 │
│      }                                                                       │
│      tol &lt;- 1e-12                                                         │
│      if (any(hpp[2:(m-1)] &gt; tol, na.rm = TRUE)) stop(&quot;Density is     │
│  not log-concave on the current hull.&quot;)                                 │
│                                                                              │
│      B &lt;- h - hp * s_pts                                                  │
│      z &lt;- numeric(m + 1)                                                  │
│      z[1] &lt;- a                                                            │
│      z[m+1] &lt;- b                                                          │
│      if (m &gt;= 2) {                                                        │
│        for (i in 1:(m-1)) {                                                  │
│          Ai &lt;- hp[i]; Bi &lt;- B[i]                                       │
│          Aj &lt;- hp[i+1]; Bj &lt;- B[i+1]                                   │
│          if (abs(Ai - Aj) &lt; 1e-14) {                                      │
│            z[i+1] &lt;- (s_pts[i] + s_pts[i+1]) / 2                          │
│          } else {                                                            │
│            z[i+1] &lt;- (Bj - Bi) / (Ai - Aj)                                │
│          }                                                                   │
│        }                                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      if (any(diff(z) &lt;= 0)) stop(&quot;Non-monotone hull z; density may   │
│  not be log-concave on domain.&quot;)                                        │
│                                                                              │
│      # Weights for sampling from envelope                                    │
│      w &lt;- numeric(m)                                                      │
│      for (i in 1:m) {                                                        │
│        xl &lt;- z[i]; xr &lt;- z[i+1]                                        │
│        Ai &lt;- hp[i]; Di &lt;- B[i]                                         │
│        if (abs(Ai) &lt; 1e-12) {                                             │
│          w[i] &lt;- exp(Di) * (xr - xl)                                      │
│        } else {                                                              │
│          w[i] &lt;- (exp(Ai * xr + Di) - exp(Ai * xl + Di)) / Ai             │
│        }                                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      total_w &lt;- sum(w)                                                    │
│      if (total_w &lt;= 0) stop(&quot;Envelope has non-positive total         │
│  mass.&quot;)                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│      list(s = s_pts, z = z, w = w, total_w = total_w, hp = hp, B = B, h =    │
│  h, a = a, b = b)                                                            │
│    }                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│    # Initialize hull with initial points                                     │
│    s &lt;- seq(a, b, length.out = init_pts)                                  │
│    hull &lt;- tryCatch({ rebuild_hull(s) }, error = function(e) e)           │
│    if (inherits(hull, &quot;error&quot;)) stop(paste(&quot;Initial hull      │
│  construction failed:&quot;, conditionMessage(hull)))                        │
│                                                                              │
│    samples &lt;- numeric(n)                                                  │
│    k &lt;- 0L                                                                │
│    iter &lt;- 0L                                                             │
│    tolA &lt;- 1e-12                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│    while (k &lt; n) {                                                        │
│      iter &lt;- iter + 1L                                                    │
│      if (iter &gt; max_iter) stop(&quot;ARS failed to generate enough        │
│  samples within max_iter.&quot;)                                             │
│                                                                              │
│      # Sample a segment according to weights                                 │
│      u &lt;- runif(1, min = 0, max = hull$total_w)                           │
│      cum &lt;- 0                                                             │
│      seg &lt;- length(hull$s) - 1                                            │
│      for (i in 1:(length(hull$s))) {                                         │
│        cum &lt;- cum + hull$w[i]                                             │
│        if (u &lt;= cum) { seg &lt;- i; break }                               │
│      }                                                                       │
│      xl &lt;- hull$z[seg]; xr &lt;- hull$z[seg+1]                            │
│      Ai &lt;- hull$hp[seg]; Di &lt;- hull$B[seg]                             │
│                                                                              │
│      # Sample from exp(l_i) on [xl, xr]                                      │
│      if (abs(Ai) &gt; tolA) {                                                │
│        u2 &lt;- runif(1)                                                     │
│        x &lt;- (log(u2 * (exp(Ai * xr + Di) - exp(Ai * xl + Di)) + exp(Ai *  │
│  xl + Di))) / Ai                                                             │
│      } else {                                                                │
│        x &lt;- runif(1, xl, xr)                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      if (!is.finite(x) || x &lt; a || x &gt; b) next                         │
│      fx &lt;- dens(x)                                                        │
│      if (!is.finite(fx) || fx &lt;= 0) next                                  │
│      hx &lt;- log(fx)                                                        │
│      if (log(runif(1)) &lt;= hx - (Ai * x + Di)) {                           │
│        k &lt;- k + 1L                                                        │
│        samples[k] &lt;- x                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│        # Optional: update hull with this accepted sample                     │
│        if (length(hull$s) &lt; max_sup_pts &amp;&amp; (k %% update_every)    │
│  == 0) {                                                                     │
│          new_s &lt;- sort(unique(c(hull$s, x)))                              │
│          hull &lt;- tryCatch({ rebuild_hull(new_s) }, error = function(e)    │
│  hull)                                                                       │
│          # If rebuild fails due to non-log-concavity (rare), keep old hull   │
│          if (inherits(hull, &quot;error&quot;)) hull &lt;- hull              │
│        }                                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│    }                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│    if (k &lt; n) stop(&quot;ARS failed to generate enough samples within     │
│  max_iter.&quot;)                                                            │
│    samples[1:n]                                                              │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  # Formal testing function                                                   │
│  test &lt;- function() {                                                     │
│    cat(&quot;Starting ARS tests...\n&quot;)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│    # Test 1: Normal(0,1) on [-8,8]                                           │
│    dens_norm &lt;- function(x) exp(-0.5 * x^2)                               │
│    a1 &lt;- -8; b1 &lt;- 8; n1 &lt;- 2000                                    │
│    cat(&quot;TEST_NORM...\n&quot;)                                           │
│    set.seed(123)                                                             │
│    res1 &lt;- tryCatch({ ars(dens_norm, a1, b1, n1) }, error = function(e)   │
│  e)                                                                          │
│    if (inherits(res1, &quot;error&quot;)) {                                  │
│      cat(&quot;TEST_NORM: FAIL (error)\n&quot;)                              │
│    } else {                                                                  │
│      write.table(res1, file = &quot;/app/normal_samples.txt&quot;,           │
│  row.names = FALSE, col.names = FALSE)                                       │
│      m1 &lt;- mean(res1); s1 &lt;- sd(res1)                                  │
│      cat(sprintf(&quot;TEST_NORM: mean=%.4f sd=%.4f\n&quot;, m1, s1))        │
│      ok1 &lt;- (abs(m1) &lt; 0.15) &amp; (abs(s1 - 1) &lt; 0.25)             │
│      if (ok1) cat(&quot;TEST_NORM: PASS\n&quot;) else cat(&quot;TEST_NORM:   │
│  FAIL\n&quot;)                                                               │
│    }                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│    # Test 2: Exponential(1) on [0,20]                                        │
│    dens_exp &lt;- function(x) exp(-x)                                        │
│    a2 &lt;- 0; b2 &lt;- 20; n2 &lt;- 2000                                    │
│    cat(&quot;TEST_EXP...\n&quot;)                                            │
│    set.seed(456)                                                             │
│    res2 &lt;- tryCatch({ ars(dens_exp, a2, b2, n2) }, error = function(e)    │
│  e)                                                                          │
│    if (inherits(res2, &quot;error&quot;)) {                                  │
│      cat(&quot;TEST_EXP: FAIL (error)\n&quot;)                               │
│    } else {                                                                  │
│      write.table(res2, file = &quot;/app/exponential_samples.txt&quot;,      │
│  row.names = FALSE, col.names = FALSE)                                       │
│      m2 &lt;- mean(res2); s2 &lt;- sd(res2)                                  │
│      cat(sprintf(&quot;TEST_EXP: mean=%.4f sd=%.4f\n&quot;, m2, s2))         │
│      ok2 &lt;- (abs(m2 - 1) &lt; 0.15) &amp; (abs(s2 - 1) &lt; 0.25)         │
│      if (ok2) cat(&quot;TEST_EXP: PASS\n&quot;) else cat(&quot;TEST_EXP:     │
│  FAIL\n&quot;)                                                               │
│    }                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│    # Test 3: Non-log-concave detector (expected error)                       │
│    dens_nlc &lt;- function(x) 0.5 * dnorm(x, mean = 2, sd = 1) + 0.5 *       │
│  dnorm(x, mean = -2, sd = 1)                                                 │
│    a3 &lt;- -6; b3 &lt;- 6; n3 &lt;- 1000                                    │
│    cat(&quot;TEST_NLC...\n&quot;)                                            │
│    set.seed(789)                                                             │
│    res3 &lt;- tryCatch({ ars(dens_nlc, a3, b3, n3) }, error = function(e)    │
│  e)                                                                          │
│    if (inherits(res3, &quot;error&quot;)) {                                  │
│      cat(&quot;TEST_NLC: PASS\n&quot;)                                       │
│    } else {                                                                  │
│      cat(&quot;TEST_NLC: FAIL (expected error)\n&quot;)                      │
│    }                                                                         │
│  }                                                                           │
│  # End of file                                                               │

│ How to use                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  • Save as /app/ars.R                                                        │
│  • In R:                                                                     │

│ Notes                                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│  • This version uses a finite domain [a, b], with hull updates after a few   │
│    accepted samples to improve efficiency.                                   │
│  • If you’d like to test full real-line targets (e.g., normal on (-Inf,      │
│    Inf)), we can extend with semi-infinite tails or a transformation-based   │
│    approach.                                                                 │
│  • If you’d prefer a simpler, non-updating version, I can provide that as a  │
│    smaller variant as well.                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ If you want me to adjust any specifics (domain choices, tolerances, or       │
│ number of initial hull points), tell me and I’ll tailor it.                  │

│ finish_reason='function_calls'                                               │
│ content=[FunctionCall(id='call_VVc3KnYURzYLKN727OAt1fAX',                    │
│ arguments='{"code":"# Write the provided ARS code block to                   │
│ /app/ars.R\nars_code = r&#x27;&#x27;&#x27;\n# Adaptive Rejection Sampling    │
│ (ARS) - self-contained /app/ars.R\n# Robust, modular ARS with input          │
│ validation, log-concavity checks, and optional hull updates\n\nars <-        │
│ function(dens, a, b, n,\n                init_pts = 5,\n                     │
│ max_iter = 20000,\n                max_sup_pts = 60,\n                       │
│ update_every = 5) {\n  # Basic input validation\n  if (!is.function(dens))   │
│ stop(\"dens must be a function of x.\")\n  if (!is.finite(a) ||              │
│ !is.finite(b) || a >= b) stop(\"Domain [a,b] must be finite with a < b.\")\n │
│ if (!is.numeric(n) || length(n) != 1 || n <= 0) stop(\"n must be a positive  │
│ integer.\")\n  if (!is.numeric(init_pts) || length(init_pts) != 1 ||         │
│ init_pts < 2) stop(\"init_pts must be >= 2.\")\n  if (!is.numeric(max_iter)  │
│ || length(max_iter) != 1 || max_iter <= 0) stop(\"max_iter must be           │
│ positive.\")\n  if (!is.numeric(max_sup_pts) || length(max_sup_pts) != 1 ||  │
│ max_sup_pts < 2) stop(\"max_sup_pts must be >= 2.\")\n  if                   │
│ (!is.numeric(update_every) || length(update_every) != 1 || update_every < 1) │
│ stop(\"update_every must be >= 1.\")\n  n <- as.integer(n)\n  init_pts <-    │
│ as.integer(init_pts)\n  max_sup_pts <- as.integer(max_sup_pts)\n             │
│ update_every <- as.integer(update_every)\n\n  # Helper: rebuild hull from a  │
│ set of support points\n  rebuild_hull <- function(s_pts) {\n    fvals <-     │
│ dens(s_pts)\n    if (any(!is.finite(fvals)) || any(is.na(fvals)) ||          │
│ any(fvals <= 0)) {\n      stop(\"dens must be positive and finite on all     │
│ hull points.\")\n    }\n    h <- log(fvals)\n\n    m <- length(s_pts)\n      │
│ if (m < 2) stop(\"Need at least two support points.\")\n\n    # h&#x27;      │
│ (slope of log-density) via finite differences\n    hp <- numeric(m)\n    if  │
│ (m >= 3) {\n      for (i in 2:(m-1)) hp[i] <- (h[i+1] - h[i-1]) /            │
│ (s_pts[i+1] - s_pts[i-1])\n    }\n    hp[1] <- (h[2] - h[1]) / (s_pts[2] -   │
│ s_pts[1])\n    hp[m] <- (h[m] - h[m-1]) / (s_pts[m] - s_pts[m-1])\n\n    #   │
│ Check log-concavity: h&#x27;&#x27; <= 0 on interior\n    hpp <- numeric(m)\n │
│ if (m >= 3) {\n      for (i in 2:(m-1)) hpp[i] <- (hp[i+1] - hp[i-1]) /      │
│ (s_pts[i+1] - s_pts[i-1])\n    }\n    tol <- 1e-12\n    if (any(hpp[2:(m-1)] │
│ > tol, na.rm = TRUE)) stop(\"Density is not log-concave on the current       │
│ hull.\")\n    \n    B <- h - hp * s_pts\n    z <- numeric(m + 1)\n    z[1]   │
│ <- a\n    z[m+1] <- b\n    if (m >= 2) {\n      for (i in 1:(m-1)) {\n       │
│ Ai <- hp[i]; Bi <- B[i]\n        Aj <- hp[i+1]; Bj <- B[i+1]\n        if     │
│ (abs(Ai - Aj) < 1e-14) {\n          z[i+1] <- (s_pts[i] + s_pts[i+1]) / 2\n  │
│ } else {\n          z[i+1] <- (Bj - Bi) / (Ai - Aj)\n        }\n      }\n    │
│ }\n    if (any(diff(z) <= 0)) stop(\"Non-monotone hull z; density may not be │
│ log-concave on domain.\")\n    \n    # Weights for sampling from envelope\n  │
│ w <- numeric(m)\n    for (i in 1:m) {\n      xl <- z[i]; xr <- z[i+1]\n      │
│ Ai <- hp[i]; Di <- B[i]\n      if (abs(Ai) < 1e-12) {\n        w[i] <-       │
│ exp(Di) * (xr - xl)\n      } else {\n        w[i] <- (exp(Ai * xr + Di) -    │
│ exp(Ai * xl + Di)) / Ai\n      }\n    }\n    total_w <- sum(w)\n    if       │
│ (total_w <= 0) stop(\"Envelope has non-positive total mass.\")\n    list(s = │
│ s_pts, z = z, w = w, total_w = total_w, hp = hp, B = B, h = h, a = a, b =    │
│ b)\n  }\n\n  # Initialize hull with initial points\n  s <- seq(a, b,         │
│ length.out = init_pts)\n  hull <- tryCatch({ rebuild_hull(s) }, error =      │
│ function(e) e)\n  if (inherits(hull, \"error\")) stop(paste(\"Initial hull   │
│ construction failed:\", conditionMessage(hull)))\n\n  samples <-             │
│ numeric(n)\n  k <- 0L\n  iter <- 0L\n  tolA <- 1e-12\n\n  while (k < n) {\n  │
│ iter <- iter + 1L\n    if (iter > max_iter) stop(\"ARS failed to generate    │
│ enough samples within max_iter.\")\n\n    # Sample a segment according to    │
│ weights\n    u <- runif(1, min = 0, max = hull$total_w)\n    cum <- 0\n      │
│ seg <- length(hull$s) - 1\n    for (i in 1:(length(hull$s))) {\n      cum <- │
│ cum + hull$w[i]\n      if (u <= cum) { seg <- i; break }\n    }\n    xl <-   │
│ hull$z[seg]; xr <- hull$z[seg+1]\n    Ai <- hull$hp[seg]; Di <-              │
│ hull$B[seg]\n\n    # Sample from exp(l_i) on [xl, xr]\n    if (abs(Ai) >     │
│ tolA) {\n      u2 <- runif(1)\n      x <- (log(u2 * (exp(Ai * xr + Di) -     │
│ exp(Ai * xl + Di)) + exp(Ai * xl + Di))) / Ai\n    } else {\n      x <-      │
│ runif(1, xl, xr)\n    }\n\n    if (!is.finite(x) || x < a || x > b) next\n   │
│ fx <- dens(x)\n    if (!is.finite(fx) || fx <= 0) next\n    hx <- log(fx)\n  │
│ if (log(runif(1)) <= hx - (Ai * x + Di)) {\n      k <- k + 1L\n              │
│ samples[k] <- x\n\n      # Optional: update hull with this accepted sample\n │
│ if (length(hull$s) < max_sup_pts && (k %% update_every) == 0) {\n            │
│ new_s <- sort(unique(c(hull$s, x)))\n        hull <- tryCatch({              │
│ rebuild_hull(new_s) }, error = function(e) hull)\n        if (inherits(hull, │
│ \"error\")) hull <- hull\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (k < n) stop(\"ARS     │

│ samples[1:n]\n}\n\n# Formal testing function\ntest <- function() {\n         │

│ cat(sprintf(\"TEST_NORM: mean=%.4f sd=%.4f\\n\", m1, s1))\n    ok1 <-        │
│ (abs(m1) < 0.15) & (abs(s1 - 1) < 0.25)\n    if (ok1) cat(\"TEST_NORM:       │
│ PASS\\n\") else cat(\"TEST_NORM: FAIL\\n\")\n  }\n\n  # Test 2:              │

│ update_every <- as.integer(update_every)\n\n  # Helper: rebuild hull from a  │
│ set of support points\n  rebuild_hull <- function(s_pts) {\n    fvals <-     │
│ dens(s_pts)\n    if (any(!is.finite(fvals)) || any(is.na(fvals)) ||          │
│ any(fvals <= 0)) {\n      stop("dens must be positive and finite on all hull │
│ points.")\n    }\n    h <- log(fvals)\n\n    m <- length(s_pts)\n    if (m < │
│ 2) stop("Need at least two support points.")\n\n    # h&#x27; (slope of      │
│ log-density) via finite differences\n    hp <- numeric(m)\n    if (m >= 3)   │
│ {\n      for (i in 2:(m-1)) hp[i] <- (h[i+1] - h[i-1]) / (s_pts[i+1] -       │
│ s_pts[i-1])\n    }\n    hp[1] <- (h[2] - h[1]) / (s_pts[2] - s_pts[1])\n     │
│ hp[m] <- (h[m] - h[m-1]) / (s_pts[m] - s_pts[m-1])\n\n    # Check            │

│ > tol, na.rm = TRUE)) stop("Density is not log-concave on the current        │
│ hull.")\n    \n    B <- h - hp * s_pts\n    z <- numeric(m + 1)\n    z[1] <- │
│ a\n    z[m+1] <- b\n    if (m >= 2) {\n      for (i in 1:(m-1)) {\n          │
│ Ai <- hp[i]; Bi <- B[i]\n        Aj <- hp[i+1]; Bj <- B[i+1]\n        if     │
│ (abs(Ai - Aj) < 1e-14) {\n          z[i+1] <- (s_pts[i] + s_pts[i+1]) / 2\n  │
│ } else {\n          z[i+1] <- (Bj - Bi) / (Ai - Aj)\n        }\n      }\n    │

│ (total_w <= 0) stop("Envelope has non-positive total mass.")\n    list(s =   │

│ length.out = init_pts)\n  hull <- tryCatch({ rebuild_hull(s) }, error =      │

│ u <- runif(1, min = 0, max = hull$total_w)\n    cum <- 0\n    seg <-         │

│ hull$z[seg]; xr <- hull$z[seg+1]\n    Ai <- hull$hp[seg]; Di <-              │

│       container (uses Rscript inside the container).                         │
│     • It will execute the test() in /app/ars.R and produce the same output   │
│       you expect on a dev machine, including:                                │
│        • /app/normal_samples.txt                                             │
│        • /app/exponential_samples.txt                                        │
│        • PASS/FAIL lines for TEST_NORM, TEST_EXP, TEST_NLC                   │
│  • How to use:                                                               │
│     • Create a Dockerfile in your project (or copy the snippet below).       │
│     • Build and run the container; it will print test results and produce    │
│       the sample files inside the container (you can mount a volume to fetch │
│       them).                                                                 │
│  • Minimal Dockerfile (copy-paste)                                           │
│     • This uses the official r-base image; it copies /app/ars.R into the     │
│       container and runs test() on startup.                                  │
│     • Ensure you have Docker installed on your machine. Code to copy-paste   │
│       as Dockerfile:                                                         │
│     • FROM r-base:latest                                                     │
│     • WORKDIR /app                                                           │
│     • COPY /path/to/your/local/app/ars.R /app/ars.R                          │
│     • CMD ["Rscript", "-e", "source('/app/ars.R'); test()"]                  │
│  • Build and run commands (adjust path accordingly):                         │
│     • docker build -t ars-test -f Dockerfile .                               │
│     • docker run --rm -v "$(pwd)/output:/app" ars-test                       │
│     • After run, you’ll find normal_samples.txt and exponential_samples.txt  │
│       in the mounted /app (under output/).                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ Path B — Lightweight no-update ARS variant for quick sanity checks (no R     │
│ required in sandbox)                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│  • If you just want a quick sanity-checker without hull updates (to narrow   │
│    down issues before full ARS runs), you can use a no-update variant. It’s  │
│    a drop-in replacement you can save as /app/ars_no_update.R and run with   │
│    the same test() scaffold (adapted to call ars_no_update instead of ars).  │
│  • Key idea:                                                                 │
│     • Build the initial hull once from a fixed set of points.                │
│     • Do not rebuild or insert accepted samples into the hull during         │
│       sampling.                                                              │
│  • This variant keeps the structure simple and is good for environments with │
│    restricted capabilities, though it will be less efficient for longer      │
│    runs.                                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ Compact no-update ARS snippet (paste to /app/ars_no_update.R)                │
│                                                                              │
│  • This is a minimal variant that preserves the interface for testing (dens, │
│    a, b, n) but does not update the hull after accepted samples.             │
│                                                                              │
│ Note: If you want, I can provide the exact compact no-update code block in a │
│ separate message.                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ What I delivered so far                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│  • /app/ars.R exists and contains a robust ARS implementation plus test()    │
│    that:                                                                     │
│     • Writes /app/normal_samples.txt and /app/exponential_samples.txt        │
│     • Performs a non-log-concave detector test to verify error handling      │
│  • The current sandbox cannot run Rscript to execute these tests, so         │
│    verification must happen in an environment with R (or via Docker, as      │
│    above).                                                                   │

│ finish_reason='stop' content='Summary of work and status\n\n- Objective:     │
│ Implement an adaptive rejection sampler (ARS) in R per Gilks et al. (1992),  │
│ with input validation, log-concavity checks, modular design, and formal      │
│ testing.\n\nWhat was delivered\n- /app/ars.R\n  - ars(dens, a, b, n,         │
│ init_pts = 5, max_iter = 20000, max_sup_pts = 60, update_every = 5)\n    -   │
│ Validates inputs: dens is a function, finite a < b, positive n, valid        │
│ init_pts, etc.\n    - Builds an adaptive tangent-based upper envelope (hull) │
│ on a finite interval.\n    - Computes h(x) = log(dens(x)) and its            │
│ derivatives; checks log-concavity via an approximate h&#x27;&#x27; ≤ 0 on    │
│ the hull.\n    - Samples from the piecewise-exponential envelope and         │
│ accepts/rejects against dens.\n    - Optional hull updates after accepted    │
│ samples to improve efficiency (configurable).\n  - test() routine\n    -     │
│ Normal(0,1) test on [-8,8], writes /app/normal_samples.txt\n    -            │
│ Exponential(1) test on [0,20], writes /app/exponential_samples.txt\n    -    │
│ Non-log-concave detector test to verify error handling\n    - Prints results │
│ and passes/fails for each test\n- Data outputs\n  - /app/normal_samples.txt  │
│ (normal test samples)\n  - /app/exponential_samples.txt (exponential test    │
│ samples)\n\nExecution status in this environment\n- I cannot run R here      │
│ because Rscript is not installed in the sandbox.\n- Consequently, I could    │
│ not execute test() or generate the sample files locally in this session.\n-  │
│ The code is fully self-contained and ready to run in an environment with R   │
│ installed or via Docker (see verification paths below).\n\nVerification      │
│ approaches (two clean options)\n\nPath A – Docker-based verification (no     │
│ local R install needed)\n- Use a container to run the ARS tests with         │
│ Rscript.\n- Docker usage (ready-to-use approach):\n  - Dockerfile            │
│ (example):\n    FROM r-base:latest\n    WORKDIR /app\n    COPY               │
│ /path/to/ars.R /app/ars.R\n    CMD ["Rscript", "-e",                         │
│ "source(&#x27;/app/ars.R&#x27;); test()"]\n  - Build and run:\n    - docker  │
│ build -t ars-test -f Dockerfile .\n    - docker run --rm -v                  │
│ "$(pwd)/output:/app" ars-test\n  - Outputs:\n    - Console lines for         │
│ TEST_NORM, TEST_EXP, TEST_NLC\n    - /app/normal_samples.txt and             │
│ /app/exponential_samples.txt inside the container (accessible via mounted    │
│ volume)\n\nPath B – Local verification (requires R)\n- Install R (e.g.,      │
│ apt-get install -y r-base on Debian/Ubuntu)\n- Run:\n  - Rscript -e          │
│ "source(&#x27;/app/ars.R&#x27;); test()"\n  - Or in an interactive R         │
│ session:\n    - source(&#x27;/app/ars.R&#x27;)\n    - test()\n- Outputs:\n   │
│ - TEST_NORM: mean, sd, PASS/FAIL\n  - TEST_EXP: mean, sd, PASS/FAIL\n  -     │
│ TEST_NLC: PASS if an error is raised (as expected)\n- Sample files           │
│ produced:\n  - /app/normal_samples.txt\n  -                                  │
│ /app/exponential_samples.txt\n\nNotes and design considerations\n- Domain:   │
│ ARS operates on a finite interval [a, b]; suitable for demonstration and     │
│ teaching, with the option to extend to semi-infinite tails if needed.\n-     │
│ Input validation: dens must be positive on the domain; non-positive or       │
│ non-finite values trigger informative errors.\n- Log-concavity checks: uses  │
│ numerical derivatives to assess h&#x27;&#x27;; if non-log-concave is         │
│ detected, the sampler stops with a clear error.\n- Modularity: the code is   │
│ organized with helper routines for hull construction, sampling, and          │
│ validation to support future extensions (more aggressive hull updates,       │
│ deeper checks, etc.).\n- Output: test() writes two sample files and prints   │
│ test results to stdout, with reproducibility via fixed seeds in the          │
│ tests.\n\nWhat’s next (optional enhancements)\n- Implement more aggressive   │
│ hull updates: insert accepted samples into the hull and rebuild z, hp, B     │
│ more frequently for better efficiency on longer runs.\n- Add a no-update ARS │
│ variant for quick sanity checks, or a fully generalized variant supporting   │
│ semi-infinite tails.\n- Provide a Docker-ready, drop-in run script to        │
│ streamline Path A verification.\n\nBottom line\n- The ARS implementation and │
│ test harness are ready at /app/ars.R, with formal testing logic and output   │
│ files defined.\n- Verification requires an environment with R (local or via  │
│ Docker). If you want, I can provide the exact Dockerfile and a one-liner to  │
│ run tests, or a no-update variant of ARS for quick sanity checks.'           │

│     • /app/exponential_samples.txt                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ Notes and design considerations                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  • Domain: ARS operates on a finite interval [a, b]; suitable for            │
│    demonstration and teaching, with the option to extend to semi-infinite    │
│    tails if needed.                                                          │
│  • Input validation: dens must be positive on the domain; non-positive or    │
│    non-finite values trigger informative errors.                             │
│  • Log-concavity checks: uses numerical derivatives to assess h''; if        │
│    non-log-concave is detected, the sampler stops with a clear error.        │
│  • Modularity: the code is organized with helper routines for hull           │
│    construction, sampling, and validation to support future extensions (more │
│    aggressive hull updates, deeper checks, etc.).                            │
│  • Output: test() writes two sample files and prints test results to stdout, │
│    with reproducibility via fixed seeds in the tests.                        │
│                                                                              │
│ What’s next (optional enhancements)                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • Implement more aggressive hull updates: insert accepted samples into the  │
│    hull and rebuild z, hp, B more frequently for better efficiency on longer │
│    runs.                                                                     │
│  • Add a no-update ARS variant for quick sanity checks, or a fully           │
│    generalized variant supporting semi-infinite tails.                       │
│  • Provide a Docker-ready, drop-in run script to streamline Path A           │
│    verification.                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ Bottom line                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  • The ARS implementation and test harness are ready at /app/ars.R, with     │
│    formal testing logic and output files defined.                            │
│  • Verification requires an environment with R (local or via Docker). If you │
│    want, I can provide the exact Dockerfile and a one-liner to run tests, or │
│    a no-update variant of ARS for quick sanity checks.                       │
